# Gemini-jailbreak

Copy and paste one of the prompts below into **DeepSeek** or **Gemini Flash-Lite** (it works only with the Flash-Lite model!).

> **Note:** Do not paste multiple prompts at once into the AI! Send them one by one.

**Success rate:** ~95%

### Prompt 1
* **Supported AI:** DeepSeek only  
